Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any viruses module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 122. The projective resolution has length at most 721 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the viruses space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 122 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 721. QED.
